RAPID KL INTEGRATED FARE TABLE - FULL STATION-TO-STATION FARES
Covers the 156 stations of the integrated Rapid KL rail network: LRT Kelana
Jaya Line, LRT Ampang/Sri Petaling Line, KL Monorail, BRT Sunway Line,
MRT Kajang Line and MRT Putrajaya Line.
Each fare is shown as CASH fare / CASHLESS fare in RM (Malaysian Ringgit).
Cash means token or paper ticket bought at the station; cashless means
Touch n Go card or open payment (debit/credit card) and is always the same
or cheaper. Concession fares (registered senior citizens, OKU, students)
are roughly half of the standard fare - see the official Rapid KL
concession fare documents for exact values.

NOTE ON THE LRT SHAH ALAM LINE (LRT3): the Shah Alam Line is NOT part of
this integrated fare matrix. Its station-to-station fares are published in
a separate fare table (see the LRT3 Shah Alam Line fare table document).
For a journey that combines the Shah Alam Line with the rest of the network
(for example transferring at Bandar Utama), the total price is the Shah
Alam Line fare plus the integrated network fare below.

Source: official Rapid KL integrated fare data (fare calculator dataset,
July 2026), cross-validated against the published Rapid KL fare table
images: matrix symmetry, completeness and cashless-not-above-cash checks
all passed; ~280 cells spot-checked against the official images.
